\varepsilon\!\in\!(0,1)
that

{\bf P}
-a.s.
R_{n}\!:=\!{\bf 1}_{\{|V_{n,s_{n}}-s|>\varepsilon\}}\to 0
and Assumption
(b)
allows us to apply Lemma
C.1
, which entails
(d^{\prime})
.
\hfill\square

So we prove Proposition
3.5
if we prove the following.

Proposition C.3
.

We keep the previous notation and we assume
(a
-
c)
and
(d^{\prime})
. Then for all
\lambda\!\in\!\mathbb{R}
,
{\bf P}
-almost surely,
\lim_{n\to\infty}{\bf E}[\exp(i\lambda M_{n,s_{n}})|\mathcal{F}_{n,0}]\!=\!\exp(-\frac{{}_{1}}{{}^{2}}s\lambda^{2})
.

Proof.
We first prove the proposition under the additional assumption that there exists
c\!\in\!(0,\infty)
such that
{\bf P}(V_{n,s_{n}}\!\leq\!c)\!=\!1
. We rely on the following standard inequalities that hold for all
a,b\!\in\!\mathbb{R}_{+}
, all
x\!\in\!\mathbb{R}
and all
z\!\in\!\mathbb{C}
:

\!\!\!\!\!\!\sup_{\hskip 8.19447pty\in[-a,a]}\!\!\!\!\!\!|e^{by}\!-\!1|\!\leq\!e^{ba}\!-\!1,\quad\big|e^{ix}\!-\!1\!-\!ix+\!\frac{{}_{{}_{1}}}{{}^{{}^{2}}}x^{2}|\!\leq\!|x^{2}|\!\wedge\!(\frac{{}_{{}_{1}}}{{}^{{}^{6}}}|x|^{3}),\quad|e^{z}\!-\!1\!-\!z|\!\leq\!\frac{{}_{{}_{1}}}{{}^{{}^{2}}}|z|^{2}e^{(\mathtt{Re}(z))_{+}}.

(3.1)

We first observe that
\alpha_{n}\!=\!{\bf E}[e^{i\lambda M_{n,s_{n}}}|\mathcal{F}_{n,0}]\!-\!e^{-\frac{{}_{1}}{{}^{2}}s\lambda^{2}}\!=\!\beta_{n}+\gamma_{n}
where

\beta_{n}\!=\!{\bf E}\big[e^{i\lambda M_{n,s_{n}}}\big(1\!-\!e^{\frac{{}_{1}}{{}^{2}}\lambda^{2}(V_{n,s_{n}}-s)}\big)\big|\mathcal{F}_{n,0}\big]\quad\textrm{and}\quad\gamma_{n}\!=\!{\bf E}\big[e^{-\frac{{}_{1}}{{}^{2}}s\lambda^{2}}\big(e^{i\lambda M_{n,s_{n}}+\frac{{}_{1}}{{}^{2}}\lambda^{2}V_{n,s_{n}}}\!-\!1\big)\big|\mathcal{F}_{n,0}\big].

We fix
\varepsilon\!\in\!(0,1)
such that
\frac{{}_{{}_{1}}}{{}^{{}^{3}}}|\lambda|\varepsilon\!<\!1
and we bound
\beta_{n}
and
\gamma_{n}
.
Since
V_{n,s_{n}}
is bounded by the constant
c
, the first inequality in (
3.1
) implies

|\beta_{n}|\leq e^{\frac{{}_{1}}{{}^{2}}\lambda^{2}\varepsilon}\!-\!1+\big(1+e^{\frac{{}_{1}}{{}^{2}}\lambda^{2}(c+s)}\big){\bf E}\big[{\bf 1}_{\{|V_{n,s_{n}}-s|>\varepsilon\}}\big|\mathcal{F}_{n,0}\big]\;.

(3.2)

Thus, by
(d^{\prime})
, for all
\varepsilon\!\in\!(0,1)
we a.s. get
\limsup_{n\to\infty}|\beta_{n}|\!\leq\!e^{\frac{{}_{1}}{{}^{2}}\lambda^{2}\varepsilon}\!-\!1
and thus a.s.
\beta_{n}\!\to\!0
.

We next observe that

\displaystyle e^{\frac{{}_{1}}{{}^{2}}\lambda^{2}s}\gamma_{n}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\sum_{\hskip 8.19447pt1\leq k\leq s_{n}}\!\!\!\!\!\!{\bf E}\Big[e^{i\lambda M_{n,k-1}+\frac{{}_{1}}{{}^{2}}\lambda^{2}V_{n,k}}{\bf E}\big[e^{i\lambda D_{n,k}}\!-\!e^{-\frac{{}_{1}}{{}^{2}}\lambda^{2}Q_{n,k}}\big|\mathcal{F}_{n,k-1}\big]\Big|\mathcal{F}_{n,0}\Big]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\sum_{\hskip 8.19447pt1\leq k\leq s_{n}}\!\!\!\!\!\!{\bf E}\big[e^{i\lambda M_{n,k-1}+\frac{{}_{1}}{{}^{2}}\lambda^{2}V_{n,k}}\big(\delta_{n,k}^{{}_{(1)}}+\delta_{n,k}^{{}_{(2)}}\big)\big|\mathcal{F}_{n,0}\big],\quad\textrm{where}

\delta_{n,k}^{{}_{(1)}}={\bf E}\big[e^{i\lambda D_{n,k}}\!-\!1-i\lambda D_{n,k}+\tfrac{{}_{1}}{{}^{2}}\lambda^{2}D^{2}_{n,k}\big|\mathcal{F}_{n,k-1}\big]\quad\textrm{and}\quad\delta_{n,k}^{{}_{(2)}}\!=\!1\!-\!\tfrac{{}_{1}}{{}^{2}}\lambda^{2}Q_{n,k}-e^{-\frac{{}_{1}}{{}^{2}}\lambda^{2}Q_{n,k}},

since
\lambda M_{n,k-1}+\frac{{}_{1}}{{}^{2}}\lambda^{2}V_{n,k}
is
\mathcal{F}_{n,k-1}
-measurable, since a.s.
{\bf E}[D_{n,k}\big|\mathcal{F}_{n,k-1}]\!=\!0
and by definition of
Q_{n,k}
. We first bound
\delta_{n,k}^{{}_{(1)}}
by use of the second inequality in (
3.1
). To that end we set

Z\!=\!\lambda^{2}D^{2}_{n,k}(1\wedge\frac{{}_{{}_{1}}}{{}^{{}^{6}}}|\lambda D_{n,k}|)
. Since

\frac{{}_{{}_{1}}}{{}^{{}^{3}}}|\lambda|\varepsilon\!<\!1
, we observe that

Z\!\leq\!\lambda^{2}D^{2}_{n,k}{\bf 1}_{\{|D_{n,k}|>\varepsilon\}}+\frac{{}_{1}}{{}^{6}}|\lambda|^{3}\varepsilon D^{2}_{n,k}
.
Therefore
|\delta_{n,k}^{{}_{(1)}}|\!\leq\!\lambda^{2}{\bf E}\big[D^{2}_{n,k}{\bf 1}_{\{|D_{n,k}|>\varepsilon\}}\big|\mathcal{F}_{n,k-1}\big]+\frac{{}_{1}}{{}^{6}}|\lambda|^{3}\varepsilon Q_{n,k}
. By the third inequality in (
3.1
), we get
|\delta_{n,k}^{{}_{(2)}}|\!\leq\!\tfrac{{}_{1}}{{}^{8}}\lambda^{4}Q^{2}_{n,k}
. Then observe that
Q_{n,k}\leq\varepsilon^{2}+{\bf E}\big[D^{2}_{n,k}{\bf 1}_{\{|D_{n,k}|>\varepsilon\}}\big|\mathcal{F}_{n,k-1}\big]
. Thus
|\delta_{n,k}^{{}_{(2)}}|\!\leq\!\tfrac{{}_{1}}{{}^{8}}\lambda^{4}Q_{n,k}\varepsilon^{2}+\tfrac{{}_{1}}{{}^{8}}\lambda^{4}c{\bf E}\big[D^{2}_{n,k}{\bf 1}_{\{|D_{n,k}|>\varepsilon\}}\big|\mathcal{F}_{n,k-1}\big]
, since
Q_{n,k}\!\leq\!V_{n,s_{n}}\!\leq\!c
. Thus,

\displaystyle e^{-\frac{{}_{1}}{{}^{2}}\lambda^{2}c}e^{\frac{{}_{1}}{{}^{2}}\lambda^{2}s}|\gamma_{n}|

\displaystyle\leq\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\!\sum_{\hskip 8.19447pt1\leq k\leq s_{n}}\!\!\!\!\!\!{\bf E}\Big[e^{\frac{{}_{1}}{{}^{2}}\lambda^{2}(V_{n,k}-c)}\big(|\delta_{n,k}^{{}_{(1)}}|+|\delta_{n,k}^{{}_{(2)}}|\big)\Big|\mathcal{F}_{n,0}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq\tfrac{1}{24}|\lambda|^{3}\varepsilon\big(4+3|\lambda|\varepsilon\big){\bf E}[V_{n,s_{n}}|\mathcal{F}_{n,0}]+\tfrac{1}{8}\lambda^{2}(8+c\lambda^{2}){\bf E}[L_{n,s_{n}}(\varepsilon)|\mathcal{F}_{n,0}]

(3.3)

where we have set
L_{n,k}(\varepsilon)\!=\!\sum_{1\leq j\leq k}{\bf E}[D_{n,j}^{2}{\bf 1}_{\{|D_{n,j}|>\varepsilon\}}|\mathcal{F}_{n,k-1}]
. Arguing as in Remark
C.2
, Assumptions
(b)
and
(c)
allow us to apply Lemma
C.1
, which entails that
\lim_{n\to\infty}{\bf E}[L_{n,s_{n}}(\varepsilon)|\mathcal{F}_{n,0}]\!=\!0
. Moreover, since
V_{n,s_{n}}\!\leq\!c
, for all
\varepsilon\!\in\!(0,1)
we a.s. get
\limsup_{n\to\infty}|\gamma_{n}|\leq\frac{1}{{24}}ce^{-\frac{{}_{1}}{{}^{2}}\lambda^{2}(s-c)}|\lambda|^{3}\big(4+3|\lambda|\varepsilon\big)\varepsilon
and thus a.s.
\gamma_{n}\!\to\!0
. We have proved that a.s.
\lim_{n\to\infty}{\bf E}[e^{i\lambda M_{n,s_{n}}}|\mathcal{F}_{n,0}]\!=\!e^{-\frac{{}_{1}}{{}^{2}}\lambda^{2}s}
under the assumption that
{\bf P}(V_{n,s_{n}}\!\leq\!c)\!=\!1
for all
n\in\mathbb{N}
.

Let us remove this assumption. We fix
c\!\in\!(1+s,\infty)
and we set
\widetilde{D}_{n,k}\!=\!D_{n,k}{\bf 1}_{\{V_{n,k}\leq c\}}
,
\widetilde{M}_{n,k}\!=\!\sum_{1\leq j\leq k}\widetilde{D}_{n,j}
,
\widetilde{V}_{n,k}\!=\!\sum_{1\leq j\leq k}{\bf E}[\widetilde{D}_{n,j}^{2}|\mathcal{F}_{n,j-1}]
,
\widetilde{L}_{n,k}(\varepsilon)\!=\!\sum_{1\leq j\leq k}{\bf E}[\widetilde{D}_{n,j}^{2}{\bf 1}_{\{|\widetilde{D}_{n,j}|>\varepsilon\}}|\mathcal{F}_{n,k-1}]
and
T_{n}\!=\!\max\{k\!\in\![\![0,s_{n}]\!]:V_{n,k}\!\leq\!c\}
. We easily check that the
\widetilde{D}_{n,k}
satisfy
(a)
since
V_{n,k}
is
\mathcal{F}_{n,k-1}
-measurable. Then, observe that

\widetilde{V}_{n,k}\!=\!V_{n,k\wedge T_{n}}
(which implies
{\bf P}\big(\widetilde{V}_{n,s_{n}}\!\leq\!c\big)\!=\!1
)
and that
\widetilde{L}_{n,k}(\varepsilon)\!=\!L_{n,k\wedge T_{n}}(\varepsilon)
.
Therefore the
\widetilde{D}_{n,k}
satisfy
(c)
because
\widetilde{L}_{n,s_{n}}(\varepsilon)\!\leq\!L_{n,s_{n}}(\varepsilon)\!\to\!0
a.s.
Next, note that for all
\varepsilon\!\in\!(0,1)
, a.s.

{\bf E}\big[{\bf 1}_{\{|\widetilde{V}_{n,s_{n}}-s|>\varepsilon\}}|\mathcal{F}_{n,0}\big]\leq{\bf E}\big[{\bf 1}_{\{T_{n}<s_{n}\}}|\mathcal{F}_{n,0}\big]+{\bf E}\big[{\bf 1}_{\{|V_{n,s_{n}}-s|>\varepsilon\}}|\mathcal{F}_{n,0}\big]\leq 2{\bf E}\big[{\bf 1}_{\{|V_{n,s_{n}}-s|>\varepsilon\}}|\mathcal{F}_{n,0}\big]

since
T_{n}\!<\!s_{n}
implies that
V_{n,s_{n}}\!>\!c\!>\!s+1
. This proves that
the
\widetilde{D}_{n,k}
satisfy
(d^{\prime})
and thus we get a.s.
\lim_{n\to\infty}{\bf E}[e^{i\lambda\widetilde{M}_{n,s_{n}}}|\mathcal{F}_{n,0}]\!=\!e^{-\frac{{}_{1}}{{}^{2}}\lambda^{2}s}
, by the previous arguments. But observe that

\displaystyle\Big|{\bf E}\big[e^{i\lambda\widetilde{M}_{n,s_{n}}}\big|\mathcal{F}_{n,0}\big]\!-\!{\bf E}\big[e^{i\lambda M_{n,s_{n}}}\big|\mathcal{F}_{n,0}\big]\Big|

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle{\bf E}\Big[\big|e^{i\lambda\widetilde{M}_{n,s_{n}}}\!-\!e^{i\lambda M_{n,s_{n}}}\big|{\bf 1}_{\{T_{n}<s_{n}\}}\Big|\mathcal{F}_{n,0}\Big]

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle 2{\bf E}\big[{\bf 1}_{\{|V_{n,s_{n}}-s|>\varepsilon\}}|\mathcal{F}_{n,0}\big]\underset{n\to\infty}{-\!\!\!-\!\!\!\longrightarrow}0

{\bf P}
-a.s. which implies the desired result.
\hfill\blacksquare
